# Skills · P · part 79

- **pyt-update-architecture**: Use when the architecture documentation must reflect recent changes â€” after an ADR is accepted, new service types added, or new infrastructure providers introduced ([juankmvanegas/factoria-powers](https://github.com/juankmvanegas/factoria-powers/tree/HEAD/skills/pyt/update-architecture))
- **pyt-update-factory**: Use when the Factoria plugin needs to be updated to the latest version with new skills, ADRs, or policies ([juankmvanegas/factoria-powers](https://github.com/juankmvanegas/factoria-powers/tree/HEAD/skills/pyt/update-factory))
- **pyt-verify-logic**: Use when business logic from a legacy system needs to be verified against the migrated implementation ([juankmvanegas/factoria-powers](https://github.com/juankmvanegas/factoria-powers/tree/HEAD/skills/pyt/verify-logic))
- **pytcr-bulk-data-loading**: Load and standardize bulk TCR-Seq / BCR-Seq repertoire data (e.g. Adaptive ImmunoSEQ, MiXCR exports) into the pyTCR standard table format, combine multiple per-sample files into one repertoire table, and compute the derived columns (frequency, clonotype count) that every bulk pyTCR analysis depends on. Use before any pytcr-bulk-* analysis skill. This is the bulk-repertoire counterpart to pytcr-data-loading, which is for single-cell AIRR data. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-bulk-data-loading))
- **pytcr-bulk-gene-motifs**: Analyze V/D/J gene usage (heatmaps, clustered heatmaps, V-J Sankey diagrams) and CDR3 amino-acid/nucleotide motif enrichment in bulk TCR-Seq repertoires with pyTCR. Use after pytcr-bulk-data-loading, once the standardized repertoire table (df) is available. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-bulk-gene-motifs))
- **pytcr-bulk-repertoire-metrics**: Compute and visualize per-sample bulk TCR-Seq repertoire metrics with pyTCR — basic counts, clonality (clonal proportion, relative-abundance clonotype groups), and diversity indices (Shannon-Wiener, Simpson, D50, Chao1, Gini). Use after pytcr-bulk-data-loading, once the standardized repertoire table (df) is available. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-bulk-repertoire-metrics))
- **pytcr-bulk-repertoire-overlap**: Compare clonotype repertoires pairwise across many bulk TCR-Seq samples with pyTCR — Jaccard index, overlap coefficient, Morisita-Horn index, Tversky index, cosine similarity, Pearson correlation, relative overlap diversity, geometric-mean overlap frequencies, and Jensen-Shannon divergence of V-gene usage, each as a sample-by-sample heatmap. Use after pytcr-bulk-data-loading, once the standardized repertoire table (df) has 2+ samples. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-bulk-repertoire-overlap))
- **pytcr-clonotype-analysis**: Analyze defined clonotypes in a scirpy MuData object — clonal expansion, clonotype abundance across categories, and convergent evolution. Use after pytcr-clonotype-clustering, once clonotype and/or clonotype cluster IDs exist in mdata.obs. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-clonotype-analysis))
- **pytcr-clonotype-clustering**: Define clonotypes and clonotype clusters from AIRR data using scirpy. Use after pytcr-preprocess. Covers nucleotide-identity clonotype definition, amino-acid similarity clonotype clustering (tcrdist), network layout/visualization, and optional V-gene-constrained clustering. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-clonotype-clustering))
- **pytcr-data-loading**: Load single-cell immune receptor (AIRR/TCR/BCR) data into scirpy. Unite with scRNA-seq in a single MuData object. Basic AnnData/MuData manipulations. Use when importing CellRanger, TraCeR, BraCeR, BD Rhapsody, or AIRR-rearrangement data, or combining multiple samples for scirpy analysis. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-data-loading))
- **pytcr-epitope-databases**: Query epitope reference databases (e.g. IggyTop, aggregating IEDB/VDJdb) to annotate cells with antigen specificity by CDR3 sequence identity. Use after pytcr-preprocess (clonotype clustering not required first). ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-epitope-databases))
- **pytcr-gene-motifs**: Analyze CDR3 amino-acid motifs and V(D)J gene usage in a scirpy MuData object — logoplots, V-gene abundance bar charts, VDJ usage Sankey diagrams, and CDR3-length spectratype plots. Use after pytcr-clonotype-clustering, once clonotype/cluster IDs and cell-type annotations exist in mdata.obs. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-gene-motifs))
- **pytcr-gex-integration**: Integrate gene expression (GEX) data with immune-receptor clonotypes in a scirpy MuData object — clonotype modularity, clonotype imbalance between cell clusters, repertoire overlap between cell types, and marker-gene / differential-expression analysis of top clonotypes. Use after pytcr-clonotype-clustering and pytcr-clonotype-analysis, once clonotype IDs, a GEX embedding, and cell-type cluster annotations exist in mdata. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-gex-integration))
- **pytcr-preprocess**: Preprocess and quality-control AIRR (TCR/BCR) data in a scirpy MuData object. Use after loading data with pytcr-data-loading. Covers chain indexing, chain QC, multichain/orphan filtering, and MuData synchronization. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-preprocess))
- **pytcr-repertoire-comparison**: Compare adaptive immune receptor repertoires across samples in a scirpy MuData object — clonotype overlap matrices, Jaccard-distance heatmaps with hierarchical clustering, and pairwise sample scatterplots. Use after pytcr-clonotype-clustering, once clonotype IDs and a sample-identifying column exist in mdata.obs. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-repertoire-comparison))
- **pytcr-sc-rnaseq-preprocessing**: Quality-control, normalize, and select features for the gene expression data (scRNA-seq). Mito/ribo/hemoglobin QC metrics, cell/gene filtering, doublet detection, count normalization (median + log1p), and highly-variable-gene selection. Use after pytcr-data-loading whenever mdata has a gex modality, independently of (and typically before or alongside) pytcr-preprocess. Should be run before pytcr-gex-integration. ([ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent](https://github.com/ALEGATOR1209/immunogenomics-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/pytcr-sc-rnaseq-preprocessing))
